  get filteredProviders(){
    let q=(this.q||'').toLowerCase();
    let ps=(this.providerSearch||'').toLowerCase();
    let cat=this.providerCategoryFilter||'';
    let list=this.providers.filter(p=>{
      if(q&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))return false;
      if(ps&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(ps))return false;
      if(cat&&p.category!==cat)return false;
      return true;
    });
    let field=this.providerSortField||'name';
    let dir=this.providerSortDir||'asc';
    list=[...list].sort((a,b)=>{
      let va=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(a.id):String(a[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      let vb=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(b.id):String(b[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      if(va<vb)return dir==='asc'?-1:1;
      if(va>vb)return dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      return 0;
    });
    return list;

  get filteredProviders(){
    let q=(this.q||'').toLowerCase();
    let ps=(this.providerSearch||'').toLowerCase();
    let cat=this.providerCategoryFilter||'';
    let list=this.providers.filter(p=>{
      if(q&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))return false;
      if(ps&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(ps))return false;
      if(cat&&p.category!==cat)return false;
      return true;
    });
    let field=this.providerSortField||'name';
    let dir=this.providerSortDir||'asc';
    list=[...list].sort((a,b)=>{
      let va=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(a.id):String(a[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      let vb=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(b.id):String(b[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      if(va<vb)return dir==='asc'?-1:1;
      if(va>vb)return dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      return 0;
    });
    return list;
  },

  get filteredProviders(){

    let ps=(this.providerSearch||'').toLowerCase();

    let list=this.providers.filter(p=>{
      if(q&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))return false;

      if(cat&&p.category!==cat)return false;
      return true;
    });

    let dir=this.providerSortDir||'asc';
    list=[...list].sort((a,b)=>{
      let va=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(a.id):String(a[field]||'').toLowerCase();

      if(va<vb)return dir==='asc'?-1:1;
      if(va>vb)return dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      return 0;
    });
    return list;

  get annualTopProviders(){let map={};this.yearlyRows.forEach(s=>{let id=s.providerId,p=this.providers.find(x=>Number(x.id)===Number(id))||{};if(!map[id])map[id]={id,name:p.name||'-',category:p.category||s.category,count:0,total:0,open:0};if(Number(s.paidValue||0)>0)map[id].count++;map[id].total+=Number(s.paidValue||0);map[id].open+=this.balance(s)});return Object.values(map).filter(x=>x.total>0||x.open>0).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total).slice(0,10)},
  get annualCategorySummary(){let rows=this.yearlyRows,total=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0)||1;return this.categories.map(category=>{let val=rows.filter(s=>s.category===category).reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0);return{category,total:val,percent:Math.min(100,Math.round(val/total*100))}}).filter(x=>x.total>0).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total)},

  get filteredProviders(){
    let q=(this.q||'').toLowerCase();
    let ps=(this.providerSearch||'').toLowerCase();
    let cat=this.providerCategoryFilter||'';
    let list=this.providers.filter(p=>{
      if(q&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))return false;
      if(ps&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(ps))return false;
      if(cat&&p.category!==cat)return false;
      return true;
    });
    let field=this.providerSortField||'name';
    let dir=this.providerSortDir||'asc';
    list=[...list].sort((a,b)=>{
      let va=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(a.id):String(a[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      let vb=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(b.id):String(b[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      if(va<vb)return dir==='asc'?-1:1;
      if(va>vb)return dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      return 0;
    });
    return list;
  },
  get filteredServices(){return this.services.filter(s=>(this.serviceFilter==='todos'||s.status===this.serviceFilter)&&(this.serviceCategoryFilter==='todas'||s.category===this.serviceCategoryFilter)&&(this.serviceProviderFilter==='todos'||Number(s.providerId)===Number(this.serviceProviderFilter))&&(!this.serviceMonthFilter||s.reference===this.serviceMonthFilter)).sort((a,b)=>String(b.serviceDate).localeCompare(String(a.serviceDate)))},

  get paymentSummary(){let rows=this.filteredPayments, paid=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0), expected=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.amount||0),0), open=rows.reduce((a,s)=>a+this.balance(s),0), paidCount=rows.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0).length, missingReceipt=rows.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0&&this.reportReceiptNumber(s)==='-').length;return{rows,expected,paid,open,paidCount,missingReceipt,overdue:rows.filter(s=>s.status!=='Pago'&&s.dueDate<this.today()).length,today:rows.filter(s=>s.status!=='Pago'&&s.dueDate===this.today()).length}},
  get paymentRecent(){return this.services.filter(s=>Number(s.paidValue||0)>0).sort((a,b)=>String(b.paymentDate||'').localeCompare(String(a.paymentDate||''))).slice(0,8)},

  get servicePanelOpenRows(){return this.servicePanelRows.filter(s=>this.balance(s)>0).sort((a,b)=>String(a.dueDate||'2999-12-31').localeCompare(String(b.dueDate||'2999-12-31')))},
  get servicePanelTotals(){let expected=this.servicePanelRows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.amount||0),0),paid=this.servicePanelRows.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||0),0);return{expected,paid,open:Math.max(0,expected-paid)}},
  get servicePanelRecurringGenerated(){return this.recurrences.filter(r=>r.active&&this.recurrenceGenerated(r,this.servicePanelMonth))},

  get categorySummary(){let total=this.services.reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||s.amount||0),0)||1;return this.categories.map(category=>{let val=this.services.filter(s=>s.category===category).reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.paidValue||s.amount||0),0);return{category,total:val,percent:Math.min(100,Math.round(val/total*100))}}).filter(x=>x.total>0).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total)},
